*Yuheng Zong,Minghua Wang,Xin Zhao,Zhi-Hui Zhan,Antonio Plaza,Jon Atli Benediktsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“先填补再推进”（FBA）策略，针对遥感多模态大模型在特定场景（如港口）能力不足的问题，通过三步渐进式训练（语义锚定→域桥融合→场景微调）填补能力缺口，在HarborEval基准上相比直接微调提升显著（LLaVA-v1.5从57.95到70.29，Qwen3-VL从81.09到83.37）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感多模态大模型（RS-MLLMs）虽提升了通用航拍图像理解能力，但在特定地球观测场景（如沿海港口）中缺乏细粒度专业化能力，受限于高质量场景数据稀缺和能力覆盖不全。现有单阶段监督微调（SFT）直接适配目标域效果不佳。

Method: 提出FBA（Filling Before Advancing）框架，将场景适配建模为“能力缺口驱动的后训练”问题。核心是三步渐进式训练：1) RS语义锚定（视觉-语言对齐）；2) 域桥融合（跨模态共享先验）；3) 证据驱动的场景微调。同时构建了CPRS三层监督数据集和HarborEval八维度诊断基准。

Result: 在HarborEval上，LLaVA-v1.5从直接微调的57.95提升至FBA的70.29（+12.34），Qwen3-VL从81.09提升至83.37（+2.28）。FBA优于Collapsed-SFT，并在港口相关的VRSBench/RSVQA子集和OpenEval上表现领先。

Conclusion: FBA通过渐进式能力缺口填补替代单阶段微调，有效提升了RS-MLLM在特定场景的细粒度专业化能力，验证了各阶段的不同作用，为遥感领域多模态模型的高效场景适配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Remote sensing multimodal large language models (RS-MLLMs) have improved general aerial-image understanding. However, Earth observation applications require fine-grained scenario specialization, constrained by scarce high-quality scenario data and incomplete capability coverage. We formulate this adaptation as a capability-gap-driven post-training problem and propose filling before advancing (FBA). Rather than relying on single-stage supervised fine-tuning (SFT) over target-domain samples, FBA first fills prerequisite capability gaps before advancing toward scenario specialization. We instantiate FBA for coastal harbor understanding, a representative multi-source scenario, by constructing CPRS (Coastal-Port Remote Sensing), a three-layer supervision dataset coupled with three ordered stages: (1) RS semantic anchoring for overhead-view visual-language alignment; (2) domain-bridge convergence for shared RS priors across target and bridging scenarios under different modalities; and (3) evidence-grounded scenario tuning for downstream performance. We construct HarborEval, an eight-track diagnostic benchmark covering perception, spatial understanding, robustness, and generation. Under comparable training budgets, HarborEval increases from 57.95 with Direct-SFT to 70.29 with FBA on LLaVA-v1.5, and from 81.09 to 83.37 on Qwen3-VL. FBA also outperforms Collapsed-SFT and leads on harbor-related VRSBench/RSVQA subsets and OpenEval. Stage-wise and role-replacement analyses validate progressive gap filling and stage-specific roles. Public examples and release updates for CPRS, HarborEval, code, and trained weights are available at https://github.com/Z0ngL1ng/filling-before-advancing.

</details>


### [37] [Deep Convolutional Large-Margin $\ell_p$-SVDD for Visual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22212)
*Alireza Dastmalchi Saei,Shervin Rahimzadeh Arashloo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DLM-SVDD，一种联合学习卷积特征与显式核边界的深度大间隔异常检测框架。通过交替优化Frank-Wolfe凸对偶边界更新与CNN梯度更新，在特征学习与边界优化间取得平衡。实验证明该方法在不平衡数据下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法具有原则性的几何决策区域但依赖于固定特征，而深度检测器能学习任务特定表示却缺乏显式的间隔感知核边界。如何在异常样本稀缺且类别高度不平衡的场景下，同时学习自适应表示与可靠的决策边界，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 1) 基于大间隔ℓ_p-SVDD构建显式核边界，同时进行间隔最大化和非线性松弛惩罚；2) 提出交替优化方案：Frank-Wolfe算法更新凸对偶边界，CNN梯度更新基于边界诱导的平滑间隔违规损失；3) 分析不同核近似策略的精度-效率权衡，提出大规模异常检测的实用建议。

Result: 在多个标准基准上的大量实验表明，该方法相比基线有一致的性能提升，与最先进方法相比整体表现强劲；即使在严重不平衡的类别分布下，联合表示-边界学习方案仍然有效。

Conclusion: 联合学习深度特征与显式核边界是可行的，该方法在异常检测任务中兼具核方法的几何严谨性与深度学习的表示能力，尤其适用于类别不平衡的挑战场景。

Abstract: Visual anomaly detection requires adaptive representations and reliable decision boundaries, particularly when anomalous training samples are scarce and class distributions are highly imbalanced. Classical kernel-based methods yield principled geometric decision regions but typically operate on fixed features, while deep detectors learn task-specific representations but often fail to provide an explicit margin-aware kernel boundary. In this study, we propose DLM-SVDD, a deep large-margin novelty-detection framework that jointly learns convolutional features and an explicit kernel-based decision boundary. By drawing on the large-margin $\ell_p$-Support Vector Data Description ($\ell_p$-SVDD) approach, the proposed method performs explicit margin maximization and nonlinear slack penalization while adapting the representation to the target task. To train the proposed model, we present an optimization scheme that alternates between a Frank--Wolfe--based update of the convex dual boundary and a CNN update step operating on a smooth margin-violation loss induced by the recovered boundary. To improve scalability, we analyze the efficiency--accuracy trade-offs for different kernel approximation strategies, deriving practical propositions for large-scale anomaly detection.
  Extensive experiments on multiple standard benchmarks show consistent performance improvements over the baseline and strong overall performance compared with state-of-the-art methods while illustrating that the proposed joint representation--boundary learning scheme remains effective under severe imbalanced class distributions.

</details>


### [38] [TRaM-VSR: Importance-Aware Token Routing and Merging for One-Step Diffusion Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22231)
*Sicheng Gao,Zhuyun Zhou,Yixuan Liu,Tong Shen,Zongwei Wu,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRaM-VSR提出基于Token路由与合并的高效视频超分辨率框架，通过融合运动时序与语义相似度的token重要性评估，结合离线规划器优化网络分组，实现计算加速同时保持高保真重建与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散Transformer的VSR方法因密集时空token序列的二次计算代价而难以实用；现有效率优化方法在单步扩散模型中易导致不可逆细节损失和时间闪烁问题。

Method: 1) 融合运动敏感时序线索与语义文本相似度估计token重要性；2) 离线规划器校准重要性并指导网络块分组路由；3) 每组内将关键token保留在高保真局部流，非重要token聚合为紧凑全局流，两者均受网络深度调制。

Result: 显著加速推理，同时保持最先进的重构质量和鲁棒的时间一致性。

Conclusion: 提出了一个兼顾效率与保真度的VSR框架，利用上下文感知先验和网络级先验实现自适应token分配，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) using large-scale Diffusion Transformer (DiT) priors achieves exceptional perceptual quality but is often impractical due to the quadratic computational cost of processing dense spatio-temporal token sequences. Existing efficiency-oriented methods risk irreversible detail loss and temporal flickering, a vulnerability especially pronounced in one-step diffusion models. To address this, we propose TRaM-VSR, a Token Routing and Merging framework for adaptive token allocation, leveraging both context-aware video priors and network-level priors. First, token importance is estimated by fusing motion-sensitive temporal cues with semantic text similarity, isolating dynamic objects and structural boundaries. Next, this importance is further calibrated and adjusted by an offline planner to guide routing across optimally grouped network blocks. Technically, within each routed group, structurally critical tokens are processed in a high-fidelity local stream, while less informative tokens are aggregated into a compact global stream, both modulated by network depth and aligned with the multigranular nature of diffusion models. Extensive experiments show that TRaM-VSR accelerates inference significantly while preserving state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and robust temporal consistency. The code is available at https://github.com/Ree1s/TRaM-VSR.

</details>

*Ziyao Huang,Shunkai Li,Juan Cao,Chenyu Li,Youliang Zhang,Zixiang Zhou,Cong Wang,Yuan Zhou,Qinglin Lu,Fan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentHOI提出了一种"先思考后生成"框架，通过多智能体推理（感知、交互、运动规划）和隐式文本-运动对齐策略，实现无需显式运动输入的文本驱动人-物交互视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人-物交互视频生成方法过度依赖显式运动控制，限制了跨不同物体和交互的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 1) 提出"先思考后生成"框架，通过多智能体推理（感知、交互、运动规划）桥接高层文本意图与物理执行；2) 引入隐式文本-运动对齐策略，将文本到运动的先验知识蒸馏到视频扩散模型中。

Result: 在穿戴、骑行等具有挑战性的物体中心场景中，AgentHOI显著提升了交互自然性、物体外观保持以及对复杂文本指令的遵循能力。

Conclusion: AgentHOI通过隐式运动理解替代显式运动输入，在不牺牲可控性的前提下提升了模型的泛化能力和可扩展性。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have spurred interest in human-object interaction (HOI) video generation, which demands fine-grained control over interaction logic beyond single-subject animation. However, existing HOI methods rely heavily on explicit motion control, limiting scalability and generalization across diverse objects and interactions. In this study, we propose AgentHOI, a text-driven HOI video generation following a thinking-before-generation framework that bridges the gap between high-level textual intent and physical execution through multi-agent reasoning over perception, interaction, and motion planning. Building upon the generated interaction plans, we further strengthen text-driven motion understanding. We introduce an implicit text-motion alignment strategy that distills text-to-motion priors into the video diffusion model, enabling robust HOI synthesis without explicit motion inputs at inference. Experiments show that AgentHOI significantly improves interaction naturalness, object appearance preservation, and adherence to complex textual instructions across challenging object-centric scenarios such as wearing and riding. The code is available at https://github.com/bone-11/agenthoi.

</details>


### [40] [From level set evolution to threshold optimization: A grayscale level set framework for image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22255)
*Xingkai Li,Jiebao Sun,Fanghui Song,Zhichang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需长度正则化项的水平集快速分割框架，通过理论证明长度项会破坏|∇φ|=1性质，并利用灰度水平集将PDE演化转化为一维阈值搜索，显著提升了退化图像（如强噪声、灰度不均）的分割速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水平集方法中长度正则化项导致数值不稳定且计算成本高，亟需一种更快速稳定的退化图像分割方法。

Method: 1. 理论证明长度项影响|∇φ|=1性质，指出其在特定光滑约束下非必要；2. 定义光滑图像类并构造灰度水平集；3. 将PDE演化转化为一维阈值搜索，实现快速分割。

Result: 在多种退化图像（强噪声、灰度不均等）上验证了分割性能，计算速度在大尺度图像上具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 所提框架无需长度正则化项，通过一维阈值搜索大幅提升效率，为退化图像分割提供了新的快速解决方案。

Abstract: The segmentation of multiple degradations has been a challenging problem in the field of image segmentation. Existing level set approaches commonly adopt a length regularization term to constrain the geometric shape of the segmentation contour. However, the introduction of the length term often results in numerical instability and high computational cost. In this paper, we show that the length term is not essential under certain smoothness constraints, and theoretically prove that the presence of the length term affects the property of $|\nabla φ|=1$. Based on the finding, we define a class of smooth images, construct the grayscale level set, and propose a fast segmentation framework for degraded images, such as heavily noisy images and intensity inhomogeneous images. The framework transforms PDE evolution into one-dimensional threshold search, which has significant advantages in computational speed, especially on large-scale images. Experiments validate the segmentation performance of the proposed framework on various degraded images.

</details>


### [41] [RadSight: Towards Perceptually Reliable Multimodal Radiology Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22293)
*Jianqin Liu,Weiwei Cao,Wanxing Chang,Ruifeng Yuan,Bowen Shi,Zhilin Zheng,Xianjie Zhang,Ling Zhang,Peng Wang,Jianpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Perception-Bench（113万样本的医学多模态大模型基准测试）和RadSight（基于双2D/3D编码器的感知驱动医学多模态大模型），揭示了现有模型因底层视觉感知能力不足导致诊断不可靠的问题，并通过渐进式课程学习在六个评估维度上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学多模态大模型在视觉理解上频繁出错，且诊断不可靠的根源被忽视——即底层视觉感知（如病灶位置、大小、密度）的瓶颈问题。作者希望通过系统追溯从高层临床任务到基础视觉感知的失败链路，并构建专用基准和模型来填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 构建Perception-Bench：含113万样本，覆盖2D和3D影像，评估属性判断、空间定位、空间理解、疾病预测、异常检测、报告生成六个维度。2) 提出RadSight：采用双2D/3D编码器架构保留原始空间结构，将医学图像理解建模为视觉-语言对齐→细粒度视觉感知→临床诊断→诊断解释的四阶段渐进过程，基于837万感知语料通过渐进课程学习训练。

Result: 在Perception-Bench上，RadSight在所有六个评估维度上一致优于现有医学多模态大模型，尤其在空间定位和临床诊断方面提升显著。在公开的2D和3D医学基准上也取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 健壮的底层视觉感知是实现可靠临床理解的关键基础。现有模型的诊断不可靠性根源于对低级视觉证据的捕获失败，RadSight证明了通过感知驱动的设计和渐进学习能够有效解决这一问题。

Abstract: Medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to perform complex image understanding tasks, yet their reliability is often compromised by frequent errors in visual interpretation. To systematically trace these failures, we traverse the hierarchy from high-level clinical tasks down to fundamental visual perception. We therefore introduce Perception-Bench, a large-scale benchmark comprising 1.13 million samples that assesses medical MLLMs across six dimensions: attribute judgment, spatial grounding, spatial understanding, disease prediction, anomaly detection, and report generation, spanning both 2D and 3D radiology images. Our analysis on Perception-Bench reveals that existing MLLMs lack the ability to capture even the most basic lesion attributes, such as location, size, and density. This inability to ground clinical outputs in primary visual evidence reveals that the models' diagnostic unreliability is rooted in a critical but overlooked bottleneck in low-level visual perception. Motivated by this, we propose RadSight, a perception-driven MLLM built upon a dual 2D/3D encoder architecture that preserves native imaging spatial structures. RadSight formulates medical image understanding as a four-stage progressive process: visual-language alignment, fine-grained visual perception, clinical diagnosis, and diagnostic interpretation. The model is trained on an 8.37 million perception-oriented corpus using progressive curriculum learning. On Perception-Bench, RadSight consistently outperforms existing MLLMs across all six evaluation dimensions, with particularly strong gains in spatial grounding and clinical diagnosis. It also achieves consistent improvements on public 2D and 3D medical benchmarks, further demonstrating that robust low-level visual perception is a critical foundation for reliable clinical understanding. Code and model will be publicly available.

</details>


### [42] [fMRI2Face: A Full-HD fMRI-Video Dataset and Geometry-Guided Neural Decoding Framework for Dynamic Human Face Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22302)
*Jingyang Huo,Xiangru Huang,Chentao Shen,Yikai Wang,Yun Wang,Jianxiong Gao,Shihao Jin,Yanwei Fu,Jianfeng Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个与可控高清数字人脸视频配对的fMRI数据集fMRI-Face，以及基于几何引导的神经视频解码框架fMRI2Face，能从大脑活动重建高保真动态人脸视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fMRI人脸解码研究受限于缺乏高分辨率、可控神经数据集，且方法难以同时恢复身份特异性外观与时变面部动态。

Method: 构建fMRI-Face数据集（62,856对fMRI-视频样本），提出fMRI2Face框架：从脑信号提取Brain-derived Appearance Context（全局身份属性）和Morphable 3D Facial Control（几何引导的位姿/表情/非刚性动态），通过Neural-Controlled Video Diffusion与潜空间补全实现视频重建。

Result: 实验表明fMRI2Face在重建保真度、身份保持、面部几何和运动一致性上均显著优于代表性神经解码基线。

Conclusion: fMRI-Face与fMRI2Face为研究动态人脸感知提供了可控平台，并建立了fMRI数字人重建的新基准。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic human faces from brain activity provides a powerful way to study how the mind perceives identity, expression, and facial motion. However, progress in fMRI-based face decoding has been limited by scarce controlled, high-resolution neural datasets and by methods that struggle to recover both identity-specific appearance and time-varying facial dynamics. We present fMRI-Face, the first fMRI dataset paired with controllable full-HD digital human facial videos rendered at 1920$\times$1080 resolution. During scanning, participants watched photorealistic, background-free facial videos with controlled identity, expression, and head pose, while fMRI activity was recorded. The resulting dataset contains 62,856 paired fMRI-video samples, providing a structured resource for studying dynamic face perception and reconstruction. Building on this dataset, we propose fMRI2Face, a geometry-guided neural video decoding framework for reconstructing facial videos from fMRI signals. fMRI2Face derives two complementary neural controls from brain activity: Brain-derived Appearance Context, which captures global identity-related visual attributes, and Morphable 3D Facial Control, which provides explicit geometry-aware guidance for pose, expression, and non-rigid facial dynamics. These controls are integrated through Neural-Controlled Video Diffusion with auxiliary latent completion, enabling high-fidelity facial video reconstruction directly from brain activity. Experiments show that fMRI2Face consistently improves reconstruction fidelity, identity preservation, facial geometry, and motion consistency over representative neural decoding baselines. Together, fMRI-Face and fMRI2Face establish a controlled platform for studying dynamic face perception and provide a new benchmark for fMRI-based digital human reconstruction.

</details>


### [43] [Geometric 2D Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22325)
*Christoph Jahn,Urs Waldmann,Bastian Goldluecke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需语义信息、能在小数据集上构建装配关系场景图的方法，利用Faster R-CNN、Transformer和基于注意力图卷积网络的Siamese网络来生成组件间的邻接矩阵并表征连接关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费品生产和机器人装配中，需要让机器人理解组件之间的装配关系，但现有方法依赖语义数据且需要大规模数据集。

Method: 使用Faster R-CNN生成几何表示，通过Transformer架构生成邻接矩阵，再输入到基于注意力图卷积网络(aGCN)的Siamese网络中进行消息传递，以表征组件间的连接关系。

Result: 在玩具模型组件的装配数据集上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能在小数据集、无语义标注条件下有效构建装配关系场景图，适用于机器人装配任务。

Abstract: In production processes for consumer products, assembly instructions are essential not only for planning but also for executing the production process. Likewise in robotics, it is crucial for an assembly robot to understand how components fit together and can be assembled. To facilitate these tasks, we contribute a method for constructing scene graphs to represent and characterize assembly relationships between components. Our approach does not rely on semantic data and is capable of handling a very small dataset. To realize this, the output of a Faster R-CNN model is used to create geometric representations, which are then processed by a transformer architecture to generate an adjacency matrix. This matrix serves as input to a Siamese network that uses message passing based on an attentional graph convolutional network (aGCN) architecture to characterize the connections between the components. We validate our method on a study dataset of toy model components which can be assembled into transportation vehicles.

</details>


### [44] [SLIP: Segmentation with Low-latency Interactive Prompting for 3D Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22332)
*Baptiste Podvin,Alexandre Ancel,Flavio Milana,Chiara Innocenzi,Davide Arrigo,Federico Espinola Schulze,Guido Torzilli,Jacques Marescaux,Daniel George,Alexandre Hostettler,Toby Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLIP是一个端到端可训练的交互式3D医学图像分割框架，通过解耦图像编码与提示引导的精炼过程，实现低延迟、支持可逆提示且兼容多种编码器的交互式分割，在13个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能，并通过受控用户研究验证了其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于补丁的交互式分割方法存在交互延迟高、对连续交互响应有限、不支持可逆提示等问题，且评估多依赖模拟实验而非真实用户研究。

Method: SLIP将图像编码与提示引导的精炼解耦：图像特征一次性计算并复用，轻量级补丁记忆库维护跨补丁的交互感知分割状态，通过传播交互上下文实现预测更新，支持可逆提示且无需重算图像特征。

Result: 在13个公开数据集上达到SOTA交互分割性能，交互延迟更低、响应性更好，支持可逆提示。受控用户研究中，专家参与者对SLIP的偏好高于nnInteractive和手动分割。

Conclusion: SLIP通过解耦设计有效解决了现有交互式分割方法的延迟和交互局限问题，同时通过真实用户研究填补了人类验证的空白，展示了更强的实用性和用户偏好。

Abstract: Interactive deep image segmentation enables efficient medical image annotation by iteratively refining predictions from user prompts, such as positive and negative clicks. Recent patch-based methods, including nnInteractive, achieve strong segmentation performance but remain limited in annotation workflows by high interaction latency, limited responsiveness to successive interactions, and the lack of support for reversible prompting. Furthermore, evaluation relies predominantly on simulated rather than controlled real-user interaction studies. We present SLIP, an end-to-end trainable framework for interactive 3D medical image segmentation that decouples image encoding from prompt-guided refinement. Image features are computed once and reused, while a lightweight patch memory bank maintains an interaction-aware segmentation state shared across patches. This representation enables prediction updates by propagating interaction context throughout the image, supports reversible prompting without recomputing image features, and substantially reduces interaction latency. By separating image representation from interactive reasoning, SLIP remains compatible with a wide range of image encoders. We train a single SLIP model for general interactive segmentation across diverse anatomical structures and imaging modalities. Beyond standard simulated evaluation, we conduct a controlled prospective user study comparing manual segmentation, nnInteractive, and SLIP across three clinical annotation tasks, six expert participants, and subjective usability measures, addressing the limited human validation of interactive segmentation methods. SLIP achieves SOTA interactive segmentation performance across 13 public datasets while providing lower interaction latency, greater responsiveness, support for reversible prompting, and higher user preference than existing approaches.

</details>


### [45] [Time-Reversed Imaging: A Multimodal Benchmark and Framework for Reconstructing Past Human-Environment Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22352)
*Jorge Bacca,Kebin Contreras,Luis Toscano-Palomino,Mauro Dalla Mura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"时间反转成像"新范式，通过热、紫外、可见光等多模态残留痕迹倒推过去的人-环境交互事件，并构建了首个基准数据集TRACE-HEI，结合视觉-语言引导的扩散模型实现可行但具挑战性的逆向推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频理解和帧预测局限于"向前看"（外推/插值），无法从物理残留痕迹中推断已发生的事件。作者希望从多模态衰退痕迹中倒推过去的交互行为，开启超越瞬态观测的视觉理解新方向。

Method: 提出TRACE-HEI多模态时序数据集（热/紫外/可见光三模态同步视频）；设计多模态推理流程：先提取痕迹的结构化文本描述，再用这些描述约束视觉-语言引导的扩散模型，重建可能的过去帧。

Result: 实验表明从消退痕迹推断近期事件具有挑战性但可行，互补模态能有效降低解空间的歧义性，证明了多模态信息对时间反转成像的关键作用。

Conclusion: 本工作定义了时间反转成像的首个计算与实验基础，桥接了视觉、物理与生成式推理，为超越瞬时观测的场景理解开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce time-reversed imaging, a new paradigm that infers what just happened in a scene from fading multimodal traces. Instead of extrapolating or interpolating video frames, our goal is to infer past human-environment interactions from residual physical imprints observable in thermal, ultraviolet, and visible spectra. To study this problem, we present TRACE-HEI, the first proof-of-concept dataset for time-reversed imaging, containing synchronized tri-modal video sequences of actions such as sitting, touching, moving objects, and liquid spills, captured across diverse materials and recorded up to three minutes after contact. To establish the benchmark, we propose a multimodal inference approach that extracts structured textual descriptions of detected traces and uses them to constrain a vision-language-guided diffusion model for reconstructing plausible past frames. Experiments show that inferring recent events from fading traces is challenging but feasible when complementary modalities reduce solution ambiguity. This work defines the first computational and experimental foundation for time-reversed imaging, bridging vision, physics, and generative reasoning, and opening new directions for scene understanding beyond instantaneous observation.

</details>


### [46] [SiPhy: Single-Image Physical Property Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22355)
*Hoang Le,Joonwoo Kwon,Elkhan Ismayilzada,Yufei Zhang,Zijun Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SiPhy是一个从单张RGB图像推断物体物理属性（质量、刚度、弹性）的统一框架，通过融合3D感知视觉线索、深度信息与语言材料知识，在多个基准上超越多视图重建方法，并可作为物理理解的数据标注引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理属性推理方法大多依赖多视图重建或基于物理的监督，而单图像场景下缺乏统一框架。作者希望仅从单张RGB图像高效、准确地推断质量、密度、弹性等物理属性，以满足仿真和具身AI的需求。

Method: 从单张RGB图像出发：(1) 采样伪体素点，提取CLIP特征；(2) 将特征与VLM提出的候选材料进行对齐；(3) 采用基于部件的对比聚合器保证区域一致性；(4) 通过重量感知精化模块改善稠密物体的厚度与体积估计。

Result: 在ABO-500、MVImgNet-100和PhysXNet-100上，SiPhy取得单图像SOTA：质量MnRE比PUGS提升93%，密度MAE比NeRF2Physics降低35.5%，杨氏模量误差降低23.5%。在真实手-物交互数据上进一步验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: SiPhy证明了仅用单张RGB图像进行物理属性推理是可行的，且性能可超越多视图方法；该方法还具备作为物理理解数据标注引擎的潜力，对具身AI和仿真领域具有重要价值。

Abstract: Inferring physical properties such as mass, stiffness, and elasticity from a single image is essential for simulation and embodied AI, yet most existing approaches rely on multi-view reconstruction or physics-based supervision. We introduce SiPhy, a unified framework for single-image physical property reasoning that aligns 3D-aware visual cues, depth with language-based material knowledge. From one RGB image, SiPhy samples pseudo-voxel points, extracts CLIP features, and grounds them to material candidates proposed by a VLM. A part-based contrastive aggregator enforces region consistency, while a heaviness-aware refinement improves thickness and volume estimation for dense objects. Across ABO-500, MVImgNet-100, and PhysXNet-100, SiPhy achieves state-of-the-art single-image performance, surpassing multi-view reconstruction methods by improving mass MnRE by up to 93% (vs. PUGS), reducing density MAE by 35.5% (vs. NeRF2Physics), and lowering Young's modulus error by 23.5%. We further validate SiPhy on real hand-object interaction datasets, demonstrating its potential as a data annotation engine for physical understanding from single-view imagery.

</details>


### [47] [Active few-shot segmentation by reinforcing data selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22371)
*Chenlan Zhao,Benny Wong,Timothy F. Lundberg,Ahmed M. Elsayed,Abdallah Aljarkas,Hamad A. Aljamaan,Lynn Karam,Qianye Yang,Yipeng Hu,Claire C. Villette,Shaheer U. Saeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用强化学习框架优化小样本学习中的支持集选择，取代传统独立评分方法，通过联合优化支持集提升医学图像分割性能。在盆腔MRI数据集上证明了超越随机选择和现有最优方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动数据选择方法主要对样本进行独立评分，没有显式考虑样本间的互补性，导致支持集选择不佳，影响小样本分割模型的适应性能。

Method: 提出强化学习框架，从无标注候选图像池中，由智能体直接预测能最大化下游分割性能的支持集，实现支持集联合优化而非独立样本评分。

Result: 在跨机构盆腔MRI数据集上，该方法优于随机选择和当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 支持集的互补性对于有效适应性至关重要，强化学习在优化适应集方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Few-shot learning enables medical image segmentation models to adapt to new tasks using only a small number of labelled examples. However, adaptation performance depends strongly on which examples are selected for the support set. Effective support sets should capture relevant variation within the target domain and be informative for adaptation, with constituent samples providing complementary information. Despite this, existing active data selection approaches largely prioritise samples individually and do not explicitly account for interactions between examples. In this work, we propose a reinforcement learning framework for support-set selection in few-shot medical image segmentation, enabling support sets to be optimised jointly rather than through independent sample scoring. Given a pool of unlabelled candidate images, an agent directly predicts a support set that maximises downstream segmentation performance. Experiments on a cross-institutional pelvic MRI dataset demonstrate improvements over random selection and current state-of-the-art methods. Our findings highlight the importance of support-set complementarity for effective adaptation and demonstrate the potential of reinforcement learning for optimising adaptation sets.

</details>


### [48] [IR275K: A Benchmark for Infrared Multi-Frame Super-Resolution Toward Efficient Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22380)
*Jie Deng,Heyang Wang,Changxin Wang,Junkai Shen,Hongyi Chen,Zhiping He,Hongxing Qi,Xudong Zhang,Jianyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IR275K——一个包含594个红外视频序列、275,196帧图像的多帧超分辨率基准数据集，并设计了轻量级状态空间模型CGMamba（10.90M参数），在X4倍超分任务上达到33.19dB PSNR，验证了2D旋转位置编码对红外条件下SSM跨帧门控稳定性的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外遥感中多帧超分辨率的评估长期分散在私人数据集和临时协议上，现有基准未能充分表征红外视频特有的热对比度、传感器噪声、弱纹理及平台引起的帧间变化，缺乏统一、可复现的评估标准。

Method: 构建IR275K基准（594个序列/275,196帧，含序列级训练/验证/测试划分和X4评估协议）；提出CGMamba模型（10.90M参数），采用2D旋转位置编码（2D RoPE）与中心引导的跨Mamba融合（CGCM），实现隐式多帧重建。

Result: CGMamba在X4超分上达到33.19dB PSNR，比红外单图像超分方法高出0.35-0.52dB且计算成本更低；去除2D RoPE导致PSNR下降1.53dB并产生严重网格伪影。

Conclusion: IR275K为红外多帧超分辨率的精度-效率评估提供了可复现基础；分析表明显式空间锚定对稳定SSM跨帧门控在红外条件下至关重要，为资源受限红外传感中的空间感知SSM设计提供了具体起点。

Abstract: Efficient processing is becoming increasingly important in infrared remote sensing, where satellite constellations produce large volumes of observations under constrained detector resolution, power, and downlink bandwidth. Multi-frame super-resolution (MFSR) offers a software-based route to spatial enhancement, but its evaluation in infrared sensing remains fragmented across private datasets and ad-hoc protocols. Existing benchmarks do not explicitly capture the thermal contrast, sensor noise, weak texture, and platform-induced frame-to-frame variation that characterize infrared video. We introduce IR275K, a curated benchmark containing 594 infrared video sequences and 275,196 frames. It provides sequence-level train/validation/test splits and a reproducible X4 evaluation protocol. As an initial architectural probe, we further evaluate CGMamba, a lightweight state-space model with 10.90M parameters and 112.14G FLOPs. CGMamba combines 2D rotary position encoding (2D~RoPE) with center-guided cross-Mamba (CGCM) fusion for implicit multi-frame reconstruction. It achieves 33.19dB PSNR, outperforming infrared single-image super-resolution references by 0.35--0.52~dB at substantially lower computational cost. Ablation results show that removing 2D~RoPE from CGCM causes a 1.53dB drop and severe grid-like artifacts. This indicates that explicit spatial anchoring is critical for stabilizing SSM-based cross-frame gating under infrared conditions. IR275K provides a reproducible foundation for accuracy--efficiency evaluation of infrared MFSR methods, while the architectural analysis offers a concrete starting point for spatially aware SSM design under resource-constrained infrared sensing. Dataset and evaluation resources are available at: https://github.com/InfraRecon7/IR275K.

</details>


### [49] [Correlation-Aware and Gaussianity-Preserving Robust Latent Angular Watermarking for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22386)
*Yebin Zheng,Haonan An,Guang Hua,Zhiping Lin,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent Angular Watermarking (LAW)，通过在扩散模型的潜空间中编码水印为反极角度（±π/2），在保持高斯性的同时实现鲁棒水印，并理论证明了解码误差与潜变量范数的反比关系及水印后相关退化的闭合形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜域水印方法存在两个主要问题：(1) 破坏潜变量的高斯性，易受检测或移除攻击；(2) 破坏i.i.d.潜变量条件，导致相关性退化和生成保真度损失。作者受各向同性高斯的旋转不变性启发，提出一种保持高斯性的角度编码水印方法。

Method: LAW方法将水印比特编码为潜变量对之间的反极角度（±π/2相对于参考对）。反极（π-分离）编码最大化比特值的几何间隔。进一步提出LAW-M变体，将水印锚定在最几何稳定的潜维度上。理论上推导了解码角度误差方差与潜对范数的比例关系，以及水印后潜变量的自相关结构闭合形式。

Result: 理论分析证明：解码角度误差方差与潜对范数平方成反比（var(Δφ) ∝ 1/ρ²）；水印后潜变量的相关性被限制在稀疏的、结构化的非对角元素上，具有固定的±π/4值。

Conclusion: LAW方法通过保持潜变量的高斯性解决了现有水印方法的鲁棒性和保真度问题，理论上的相关退化表征为水印方法的可解释性提供了严格基础。

Abstract: Latent domain watermarking for diffusion models embeds watermarks directly into the latent prior, enjoying non-intrusiveness to model parameters and seamless integration with the generation process. However, due to the violation of latent Gaussianity or sensitivity to normal and malicious perturbations during latent inversion, existing methods are prone to watermark detection or removal attacks. A further overlooked problem is the violation of the i.i.d. latent condition after watermarking, which leads to latent correlation degradation and generation fidelity loss. Although this has been externally measured by FID, the internal correlation structure has yet to be rigorously characterized. To address the above issues, and motivated by the rotation-invariant property of isotropic Gaussian, we propose \textit{Latent Angular Watermarking (LAW)}, which encodes watermark bits as antipodal angles ($\pmπ/2$ relative to a reference pair) between disjoint pairs of latent elements while preserving the Gaussianity. The antipodal ($π$-separation) encoding maximizes geometric separation between bit values, and we prove that the decoding angular-error variance is proportional to the norm of the latent pair, i.e., $\operatorname{var}(Δφ) \propto 1/ρ^2$. We further propose a magnitude-driven variant, LAW-M, which anchors watermark bits in the most geometrically stable latent dimensions, yielding additional robustness gains. Theoretically, we provide a rigorous characterization of the induced correlation degradation, deriving in closed form the autocorrelation structure of the watermarked latent and proving that correlations are confined to a sparse, structured set of off-diagonal elements with fixed $\pmπ/4$ values.

</details>


### [50] [Deformable Triangle Splatting: Flexible Primitives for Real-Time Radiance Field Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22446)
*Oriol Jiménez-Ayguadé,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可变形三角形溅射（Deformable Triangle Splatting），通过在三角形每条边上引入多个可学习的控制点，使原本凸边界的2D图元能够表示非凸形状，从而更高效、高质量地重建场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于2D图元（如高斯盘、三角形）的辐射场方法受限于凸边界，无法高效表示弯曲和凹面结构，需要大量图元才能逼近。

Method: 为每个三角形的每条边增加K个控制点，每个控制点用可学习的标量位移参数表示，使其边界可向内或向外移动形成非凸形状；在重心坐标空间设计可微分光栅化管线，使用绕数测试判定像素位置，并引入窗口函数（含锐度、角平滑度两个可学习参数）实现平滑的透明度过渡。

Result: 在多种真实场景中取得优于近期基于非体积图元方法的视觉质量和泛用性，同时保持有竞争力的渲染效率。

Conclusion: 可变形三角形通过少量额外参数实现非凸形状表示，在视觉质量与渲染效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Recent radiance field methods represent scenes with 2D primitives that offer surface alignment and efficient rasterization, from Gaussian disks to triangles, yet all rely on convex boundaries: curved and concave structures demand excessive primitives. We introduce Deformable Triangle Splatting, which augments each triangle with $K$ control points per edge, each parameterized by a single learnable scalar displacement that shifts the boundary inward or outward, enabling non-convex shape representation while preserving the three base vertices that define the 3D plane. To render these non-convex primitives differentiably, we design a rasterization pipeline in the triangle's barycentric coordinate space, ensuring view-consistent rendering. A winding number test determines whether each pixel lies inside the deformed primitive, and a window function controlled by two learnable parameters, sharpness and corner smoothness, together with a per-primitive scalar opacity, produces the smooth opacity transition from interior to boundary. Validation is done in a variety of real-world scenes, outperforming recent works based on non-volumetric primitives in terms of visual quality and versatility while still achieving competitive rendering efficiency.

</details>


### [51] [Optimal Transport Image Representation and Deep Covariance Alignment (CORAL) for Control Valve Stiction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22486)
*Seshu K. Damarla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合最优传输(OT)成像与深度相关对齐(Deep CORAL)的阀门粘滞检测方法，通过对控制器输出和过程变量生成二维OT图像，并训练CNN编码器学习域不变特征，在20个工业回路基准上实现了90%准确率和92.86% F1分数，有效解决了模拟数据到真实工业数据的域迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程中控制阀粘滞是导致回路振荡和性能下降的常见原因，现有的数据驱动方法在模拟数据上训练后难以泛化到真实工业回路，存在严重的域偏移问题。

Method: 1) 将控制器输出和过程变量信号转换为二维OT图像捕获动态行为；2) 使用CNN编码器同时优化交叉熵损失(有标签模拟数据)和Deep CORAL协方差对齐损失(模拟数据与无标签目标域工业数据)，学习域不变表示；3) 在域不变特征上训练下游分类器进行检测。

Result: 在20个工业回路基准测试中，成功诊断18个；在13个粘滞案例上实现100%召回率；准确率90.00%，F1分数92.86%，显著优于基于手工特征的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了模拟数据与真实工业数据之间的域偏移，为实际工业控制回路提供了鲁棒、高可靠性的阀门粘滞检测方案。

Abstract: Control valve stiction is a common cause of unwanted oscillations and poor control-loop performance in industrial processes. Data-driven methods can automatically detect stiction, but models trained purely on simulated data often struggle to generalize to real industrial control loops due to domain shift. To bridge this gap, this work propose a novel stiction detection methodology that combines optimal transport (OT) imaging technique with deep correlation alignment (Deep CORAL) algorithm. Closed loop signals: controller output and process variable are converted into two-dimensional OT images. These images capture the dynamic behaviour of control loops. The proposed methodology includes a convolutional neural network (CNN) encoder (or feature extractor) trained to learn domain-invariant representations by optimizing a combined objective: a cross-entropy loss on labeled simulation data and a Deep CORAL (covariance-alignment) loss between simulation data and unlabeled target-domain industrial data. Downstream classifiers trained on the domain-invariant target features were evaluated on an independent test set of 20 benchmark loops from industrial stiction data benchmark. The proposed methodology successfully diagnosed 18 out of the 20 loops and achieved100% recall across all 13 stiction cases, an accuracy of 90.00% and an F1-score of 92.86%. Compared to standard baseline approach (hand-crafted features-based method), the proposed methodology significantly mitigates domain shift, providing robust, highly reliable stiction detection for real-world industrial control loops.

</details>


### [52] [Twins: Learn to Predict Unified Representations with Focal Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22531)
*Kaixiong Gong,Xin Cai,Bin Lin,Hao Wang,Yunlong Lin,Mingzhe Zheng,Bohao Li,Jian-Wei Zhang,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Twins提出一种通过通道级联ViT和VAE特征的统一连续视觉标记空间，在不增加序列长度和注意力成本的前提下，解决多模态模型中理解与生成表征不匹配的问题，并针对扩散Transformer中的优化不平衡提出了焦点回归目标函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在连续管道中通常使用两种不匹配的表征——语义特征（如ViT）用于理解，低级潜在特征（如VAE）用于生成，导致潜在空间不匹配。

Method: 提出Twins统一连续标记空间，通过通道级联ViT和VAE特征于同一标记网格；针对联合建模时的优化不平衡（频率偏差、内在维度、条件对齐与条件无关的不确定性），采用焦点回归目标函数上采样大误差VAE维度。

Result: 在ImageNet上，相比朴素MSE损失，无分类器引导下gFID提升最高达10.57；在多模态理解基准上表现有竞争力，并提升了重建保真度。

Conclusion: Twins缩小了理解导向与生成导向表征之间的差距，验证了统一连续标记空间的有效性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models seek a shared visual token space that supports both multimodal understanding and image generation. Discrete methods unify the interface via a shared codebook, whereas continuous pipelines often rely on two disparate representations -- semantic features (e.g., ViT) for understanding and low-level latents (e.g., VAE) for synthesis -- resulting in mismatched latent spaces. We propose Twins, a unified continuous token space formed by channel-wise concatenating ViT and VAE features on the same token grid, so the sequence length is unchanged and attention cost does not increase. However, jointly modeling Twins in a Diffusion Transformer exposes a severe optimization imbalance: the model fits the ViT component well but struggles to match the VAE latent distribution. We trace this imbalance to three sources of heterogeneity: frequency bias, intrinsic dimensionality, and condition-aligned vs condition-independent uncertainty. To address it, we adapt a focal regression objective for flow matching that upweights large-error VAE dimensions, better balancing optimization across the ViT and VAE components. On ImageNet, this yields up to 10.57 gFID gain over naive MSE loss without classifier-free guidance. Twins also performs competitively on multimodal understanding benchmarks and improves reconstruction fidelity, narrowing the gap between understanding- and generation-oriented representations.

</details>


### [53] [SM4RT: Learning Structured Motion Geometry for 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22534)
*Shing Ho J. Lin,Wenzhao Zheng,Dong Zhuo,Yuqi Wu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SM4RT，一种端到端的4D动态重建 Transformer，通过将场景运动分解为SE(3)中的6D扭转运动基，实现从单目RGB视频中联合推断3D几何、世界坐标运动和场景运动学结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动感知方法将运动视为独立点位移，忽略了物理世界中物体遵循刚体运动学的结构化特性（SE(3)变换），缺乏对运动几何结构的建模。

Method: 提出Structure-of-Motion表示，将场景运动分解为紧凑的运动基（每个基为SE(3)中6D扭转的时间序列），通过稀疏、时间共享的逐像素分配权重恢复稠密场景运动，并设计并行运动几何编码器-解码器实现单次前向传播联合推理。

Result: 在运动重建性能上表现强劲，同时保留了场景运动的结构化几何特性。

Conclusion: 将物理运动的结构先验（刚体运动学、SE(3)变换）引入4D重建，为几何基础模型从3D理解拓展到4D动态理解提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Geometry Foundation Models (GFMs) have substantially advanced monocular 3D reconstruction, yet extending this capability to 4D dynamic understanding remains a fundamental challenge. Most existing motion perception methods (e.g., sparse tracking, dense point-wise flow) treat motion as independent point-wise displacements, ignoring the structured nature of physical motion. However, real-world objects usually obey rigid-body kinematics, and points thus usually move collectively, not in isolation. Motion itself possesses geometric structure: physical objects undergo a set of rigid-body transformations governed by SE(3), rather than unstructured point-wise displacements. Building on this insight, we propose SM4RT, a Structured Motion 4D Reconstruction Transformer for end-to-end 3D reconstruction and structured motion perception. SM4RT introduces Structure-of-Motion to represent scene dynamics, where scene motion is decomposed into a compact set of motion bases, each represented as a temporal sequence of 6D twists in SE(3). Dense scene motion is then recovered by sparse, time-shared per-pixel assignment weights over these bases, ensuring points on the same object share a common rigid-body motion trajectory. SM4RT introduces a parallel motion geometry encoder and decoder that jointly infer 3D geometry, world-coordinate motion, and scene kinematic structure in a single forward pass from monocular RGB video. SM4RT achieves strong motion reconstruction performance while preserving the geometric structure of scene motion.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [54] [Adversarial Style Optimization: Enhancing VLM Jailbreaks by GRPO-based Stylistic Triggers Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21619)
*Bingjun Luo,Jialin Guo,Yue Yao,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大模型在内容理解与安全防御之间存在"风格不一致性"：模型能稳健理解内容，但其防御机制易被特定视觉风格绕过。据此提出对抗风格优化（ASO），通过GRPO微调图像编辑模型叠加优化后的风格修改，显著提升现有视觉越狱攻击的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于内容的越狱攻击对快速演进的MLLMs效果不佳，且未能利用非内容层面的漏洞。作者发现MLLMs在理解能力与安全能力之间存在风格层面上的不一致性，并据此探索利用风格偏差作为新的攻击向量。

Method: 提出Adversarial Style Optimization (ASO)，一个即插即用模块。通过GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）微调图像编辑模型，将优化后的风格修改叠加到已有的对抗图像上。优化过程由分层结构奖励函数引导，结合logit层面的拒绝信号和高保真语义评估。

Result: 大量实验表明ASO显著提升了最先进攻击方法的攻击成功率（ASR），证明风格偏差是红队测试MLLMs的可扩展向量。

Conclusion: 风格不一致性是多模态大模型安全对齐中的一个关键漏洞，ASO有效利用了该漏洞，为红队测试和提升模型安全性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance, but their safety alignment remains vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Existing content-based jailbreaks are often inconsistent and show unsatisfying performance against the rapidly evolving MLLMs, failing to exploit non-content-based vulnerabilities. Unlike previous research, we empirically find that MLLMs exhibit a Stylistic Inconsistency between their comprehension ability and safety ability: MLLMs can robustly understand content regardless of visual style, yet their defense mechanisms can be easily bypassed by specific stylistic triggers. Based on this finding, we propose Adversarial Style Optimization (ASO), a plug-and-play enhancement module to amplify existing visual jailbreaks. ASO fine-tunes an image-editing model to superimpose an optimized stylistic modification onto a given adversarial image, using a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) agent guided by a Structurally-Tiered Reward Function that combines a logit-based signal for detecting explicit refusals with a high-fidelity semantic evaluation from a powerful judge model. Extensive experiments show that ASO significantly enhances the ASR of SOTA attacks, demonstrating that stylistic biases are a scalable vector for red-teaming MLLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/bingjunluo/ASO.

</details>


### [55] [A Consensus-Based Framework for Relative Preference Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21632)
*Mohtashim Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于共识的评估框架，通过多LLM投票生成相对偏好排名（RII），而非依赖静态标注和绝对正确性，用于衡量多答案场景下的响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM基准依赖静态数据集和客观评分，在存在多个可接受答案的场景下无法区分清晰度、完整性和有用性等质量差异。

Method: 使用多个LLM组成评审小组，在盲条件下对彼此生成的响应进行投票排名，聚合形成相对智能指数（RII）。

Result: 跨领域存在一致的偏好模式，部分模型的响应更频繁地被同行模型评为高质量；RII反映了模型间的偏好对齐，而非客观正确性。

Conclusion: 该方法为多有效答案场景提供了一种可扩展的模型驱动比较评估视角，虽与人类判断不完全一致，但可作为代理信号。

Abstract: Traditional benchmarks for LLMs primarily rely on static datasets and objective scoring metrics, which often fail to capture differences in response quality when multiple answers are acceptable. In such settings, correctness alone is insufficient to distinguish between responses that vary in clarity, completeness, and usefulness.
  This paper introduces a consensus-based evaluation framework that measures relative preference among model-generated responses rather than absolute correctness. Instead of evaluating outputs against a fixed ground truth, we assess how a panel of diverse LLMs ranks anonymized candidate responses to the same prompt. This approach treats aggregate inter-model agreement as a proxy for perceived response quality under blind conditions.
  We conduct a controlled study using five state-of-the-art LLMs across multiple domains, including programming, general knowledge, safety, logical reasoning, and mathematics. Each model generates responses and independently ranks peer outputs through a structured voting process. Scores are aggregated into a Relative Intelligence Index (RII), representing how frequently a model's responses are preferred by other models.
  Our findings reveal consistent preference patterns across domains, with certain models more frequently ranked highly by their peers. However, we emphasize that these results reflect inter-model preference alignment rather than objective correctness or human judgment. This framework provides a scalable, model-driven method for comparative evaluation, offering an alternative perspective on response quality in scenarios where multiple valid answers exist. While not directly aligned with human evaluation, prior work suggests that aggregated model preferences can partially correlate with human judgments, motivating this as a proxy signal.

</details>


### [56] [Evaluation design conditions the expert-vs-auto MeSH gap: a controlled comparison of bag-of-words and BiomedBERT on the Cohen benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21685)
*Samuel M. Okoe-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了专家标注与自动生成的MeSH词作为分类器特征的效果差异，发现这种差异高度依赖于评估设计（如交叉验证折数、语料库大小），在特定评估设计下差距可缩小至统计不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述需要从大量摘要中筛选相关文献，分类器常使用MeSH词作为输入特征。然而专家标注的MeSH词通常滞后数周至数月，而自动工具可即时生成。此前没有直接比较两者作为分类器特征的效果，也没有研究评估设计对比较结果的影响。

Method: 使用Cohen等人(2006)的药物分类基准，在三个主题上比较词袋逻辑回归分类器（7次运行）和BiomedBERT（5个种子）的表现。核心分析聚焦Statins主题，改变评估设计（5折全语料库、匹配语料库大小、10折交叉验证），并进行了功效分析和表征不对称分析。

Result: 标准5折全语料库设计下，专家MeSH vs 自动MeSH在Statins上的差距为+0.096 WSS@95%。匹配语料库大小后降至+0.033（CI包含零），10折交叉验证降至+0.021（CI勉强排除零）。BiomedBERT给出+0.020，接近词袋模型的10折结果。15.1%的Statins输入在添加专家MeSH后超出BiomedBERT的512词元限制。

Conclusion: 特征来源（专家vs自动MeSH）的基准结论会因评估设计的合理改变而发生显著变化；在已测试的主题上，使用Transformer或10折词袋模型的筛选管道中，差距约为0.02 WSS@95%。

Abstract: A systematic review begins with someone reading thousands of abstracts to identify the few that are relevant, and classifiers are used to prioritise that reading. Their inputs are often augmented with Medical Subject Headings (MeSH), assigned either by expert indexers weeks or months after publication or by automatic tools at once. To our knowledge the two have not been compared directly as classifier features, and no previous work has asked whether that comparison's outcome depends on how the classifier is evaluated. Using the Cohen et al. (2006) drug-class benchmark on three topics, we characterise a bag-of-words logistic regression classifier (seven reruns) and BiomedBERT (five seeds), then examine how the Statins result changes under alternative designs. Under the canonical 5-fold full-corpus design, the bag-of-words expert-vs-auto gap on Statins is +0.096 WSS@95%. Matching the corpus size to the smaller topics (n = 803) reduces it to +0.033 (95% bootstrap CI includes zero), and 10-fold cross-validation at full size to +0.021 (CI narrowly excludes zero). Under canonical evaluation BiomedBERT gives +0.020, within sampling noise of the bag-of-words 10-fold result. A power analysis indicates a Statins-sized effect would not have been detectable at the Opioids or ADHD variance, so those nulls are design-limited rather than informative. A representation asymmetry remains: 15.1% of Statins inputs exceed BiomedBERT's 512-token limit when expert MeSH terms are appended, so truncation may contribute to the smaller transformer gap, although this cannot be separated from training volume here. In screening pipelines using transformers or 10-fold bag-of-words, the gap on the topics tested is about 0.02 WSS@95%, with CIs spanning zero on at least one bound. More broadly, benchmark conclusions about feature sources can change substantially under reasonable changes to the evaluation design.

</details>


### [57] [Humanly: A Configurable and Traceable Environment for Human-AI Collaborative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21758)
*Shenzhe Zhu,Haoqian Zhang,Xu Yang,Jingyu Tang,Yi Nian,Xiaoxue Du,Shu Yang,Alex Pentland,Joachim Baumann,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Humanly 写作平台，通过记录完整的写作过程（包括人类键入和AI辅助活动）生成可验证的写作证书，用于区分人类写作与AI生成内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估仅依赖最终文本，无法区分人类写作、AI生成或人机协作成果，现有过程追踪工具存在记录粗糙、环境控制有限等问题。

Method: 构建Humanly平台：用户配置写作环境，记录写作活动及AI辅助行为，生成附带配置感知异常行为审查的可密封写作证书，并开发Humanly Typing Detector区分人工与自动键入。

Result: 用户研究表明Humanly在不同角色中均有帮助；红队测试表明Humanly Typing Detector能有效区分人类手打与自动键入。

Conclusion: 通过记录完整写作过程而非仅依赖最终文本，Humanly为写作真实性验证提供了可靠方案，可应用于课程作业、同行评审、个人认证等场景。

Abstract: Teachers, conference chairs, and public readers all judge writing from limited evidence, seeing only a finished document and not the process that produced it. Final text alone cannot reveal whether a document was produced through human typing, AI generation, or mixed human-AI collaboration. Existing process-tracking tools help, but many are tied to host-document histories, provide coarse activity records, and offer limited control over the writing environment. Humanly is a writing platform that makes the writing process itself the evidence. Users configure writing environments for personal documents or assigned tasks and draft in a workspace that records writing activity and in-platform AI assistance. Humanly can package a completed session into a sealed writing certificate with configuration-aware anomaly behavior review. It can support writing scenarios such as course assignments, peer review, and personal certification. Our user study shows that Humanly is helpful across roles, and a red-teaming study shows that the Humanly Typing Detector distinguishes human hand typing from automated typing.

</details>


### [58] [Probing Latent Colombian Identity Inferences in Qwen2.5-7B with Natural Language Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21774)
*Pablo Santiago Potes Velasco,María del Mar García Matabanchoy,Óscar Julián Pérez Ladino,Jhoan Stevan Mosquera Ortiz,Nicolás Lozano Mazuera,Gilber Alexis Corrales Gallego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这项试点研究使用自然语言自动编码器（NLA）探测Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型在层20的残差流激活中是否内部表征了哥伦比亚身份、社会经济地位或刻板印象信息，发现即使不明确提及，模型也可能通过细微语言线索推断人口统计属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能从细微语言线索中推断人口统计属性，即使这些属性未被明确陈述，但缺乏对西班牙语变体中潜在偏见的激活级可解释性研究。

Method: 使用自然语言自动编码器（NLA）将残差流激活转化为自然语言，分析Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型第20层在4个位置四分位的激活状态。数据集包含30个提示（15对西班牙-英语配对），涵盖显式哥伦比亚线索、隐式哥伦比亚线索和中性控制三种条件。

Result: 报告了描述性频率和定性证据，而非统计显著效应。模型在显式和隐式哥伦比亚提示中均观察到潜在国籍或刻板印象表征在语言输出之前出现。

Conclusion: 该研究将激活级可解释性与面向资源不足西班牙语变体的偏见评估联系起来，为理解模型如何处理与拉丁美洲身份相关的语言线索提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Large language models may infer demographic attributes from subtle linguistic cues even when those attributes are not explicitly stated. This pilot study examines whether Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct internally represents Colombian identity, socioeconomic status, or stereotype-related information when processing Colombian-Spanish and English prompts. We use Natural Language Autoencoders (NLA) to verbalize residual-stream activations from layer 20 across four positional quartiles per prompt. Our dataset contains 30 prompts arranged as 15 matched Spanish-English pairs, spanning explicit Colombian cues, implicit Colombian cues, and neutral controls. We report descriptive rates and qualitative evidence rather than statistically powered effects, focusing on whether latent nationality or stereotype representations appear before they are verbalized in the model output. This work connects activation-level interpretability with bias evaluation for underrepresented Spanish varieties.

</details>


### [59] [Khondo: A Multimodal Benchmark for Document Packet Splitting of Bangla Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21780)
*Abu Tyeb Azad,Fahim Ahmed,Ishita Sur Apan,Ezharuddin Jubaer,Sumaiya Karim Katha,Armun Alam,Amin Ahsan Ali,Aman Chadha,Md Mofijul Islam,AKM Mahbubur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Khondo是首个用于孟加拉国政府表单的文档包分割基准，包含双语（孟加拉语-英语）、视觉原生数据，评估发现MLLMs在页面聚类上表现良好，但页面排序恢复困难，语言和指令主要影响排序而非聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府和工作流中常见多文档拼接成单个文件（文档包），但将其分割为原始文档很困难，尤其对低资源语言。现有工作缺乏此类基准，因此需要构建评估数据集。

Method: 构建Khondo基准数据集，包含Bangla-English双语、视觉原生（直接基于页面图像）的孟加拉国政府表单，涵盖5种拼接方案、14个行政领域。使用MLLMs进行零样本评估，并通过控制实验（变更提示指令和语言）分析模型表现。

Result: MLLMs在页面聚类（识别哪些页面属于同一原始文档）上表现尚可，但在恢复打乱后的原始页面顺序上困难较大。显式的页面顺序指令是必要但不充分的，英文包的排序比孟加拉语更可靠。

Conclusion: 页面顺序重建是视觉基础、低资源文档理解中的一个关键开放问题，Khondo为此提供了受控基准，并指出页面排序是主要挑战，语言是次要但一致的影响因素。

Abstract: Document packets, multiple documents concatenated into a single file, are common in government and administrative workflows, yet splitting them into their constituent documents is difficult, especially for low-resource languages. We introduce Khondo (Bangla for split/segment), the first benchmark for document packet splitting on Bangladeshi government forms. Unlike prior English and OCR-text-based datasets, Khondo is bilingual (Bangla--English) and vision-native; where models operate directly on page images. It spans five concatenation schemes, from sequential to fully shuffled, across 14 administrative domains, with ground-truth boundaries, domain types, and page order. Zero-shot evaluation of MLLMs shows they cluster pages into their source documents fairly well but struggle in restoring the original page order once shuffled. To isolate what drives this difficulty, we run two controlled analyses, varying the prompt instruction and then the packet language. Both primarily affect ordering rather than clustering: (a) explicit page-order instructions are necessary but insufficient, and (b) English packets are ordered more reliably than Bangla, making page arrangement the dominant challenge and language a secondary but consistent factor. Khondo establishes page-order reconstruction as a key open problem in vision-based, low-resource document understanding, and provides a controlled benchmark for measuring progress toward solving it. Our dataset and code is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Mausul/khondo

</details>


### [60] [Agentic Evaluation of Copyright Law Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21799)
*Zheng Hui,Doni Bloomfield,Noam Kolt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Copyright-Bench基准，用于评估LLM代理在商业任务中遵守版权法的情况。研究发现，先进LLM代理会在存在公有领域替代品的情况下仍选择受版权保护的作品，且开源模型在用户偏好和时间压力下违规率上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理越来越多地执行涉及检索和再现外部内容的商业任务，但缺乏评估其是否遵守版权法的框架。

Method: 构建Copyright-Bench基准，包含网站开发、商品设计和路演材料制作三类商业任务，要求代理在公有领域（合法）和受版权保护（侵权）内容间选择，并引入用户偏好和时间压力等提示变化。

Result: (1) 代理在有公有领域替代品时仍选择侵权作品；(2) 开源模型在特定用户偏好和模拟时间压力下违规率上升。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理在版权合规方面存在显著问题，尤其是开源模型更易受外部因素影响而违规，需要更完善的合规机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly perform commercial tasks that involve retrieving external content such as images and, where appropriate, reproducing that content. LLM agents should comply with the law, including copyright law. Presently, however, we lack adequate frameworks to assess whether they do so in practice. To that end, we introduce \textbf{Copyright-Bench}, a benchmark designed to evaluate \textit{LLM agents' compliance with} \emph{copyright law}. Copyright-Bench is comprised of realistic commercial tasks---website development, merchandise design, and pitch deck production---that involve agents selecting between public-domain content (the use of which is \textit{legal}) and copyrighted content (the use of which is \textit{infringing} in this setting).The evaluation introduces prompt variations that simulate different user preferences, as well as time pressure.Comparing state-of-the-art LLM agents against a human baseline, we find that: (1) agents select copyrighted works despite the availability of public-domain alternatives; and (2) for open-weights models, violation rates increase in response to certain user preferences and simulated time pressure.

</details>


### [61] [Data Quality over Capacity: Internalizing Documents into LoRA Adapters for Closed-Book QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21861)
*Joan Figuerola Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过LoRA将文档直接烘焙进4-bit Gemma模型的权重中，实现闭卷问答。发现数据质量远超架构调整的影响，单次数据筛选使准确率从57.7%提升至85.7%，且内化适配器在召回率上超越BM25-RAG流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统需要检索管道和上下文窗口，延迟高且成本大。作者希望将文档知识直接编码到模型权重中，实现无需检索、无需上下文窗口的闭卷问答系统。

Method: 使用LoRA对4-bit Gemma-4-e4b模型进行微调，将文档知识注入模型权重。探索不同适配器容量（LoRA rank）、数据质量（单次筛选：将答案缩短至1-6词，删除琐碎问题）、学习率等超参数的影响，并在99文档语料上进行约100次训练实验。

Result: 1) 适配器容量是关键门控因素，容量不足时任何数据策略都无效；2) 容量充足后，数据质量是主导因素，远超LoRA rank、学习率和两种替代架构的影响；3) 单次数据筛选使15文档语料的闭卷准确率从57.7%提升至85.7%；4) 内化适配器（84.2%召回）超越BM25-RAG（58.9%）和黄金块Oracle（65.6%），且延迟更低。

Conclusion: 模型内化知识（闭卷）可以超越传统RAG流水线（开卷），关键在于适配器容量和数据质量。同时作者记录了完整的实验弧线（包括三次误诊），作为调优LLM训练的实证案例。

Abstract: We study baking documents directly into the weights of a 4-bit Gemma-4-e4b model via LoRA, so a system can answer questions about a corpus closed-book: no retrieval and no context-window budget. Across roughly 100 training runs from single documents to a 99-document corpus, we find that once adapter capacity is adequate, training-data quality is the dominant lever on closed-book accuracy, outweighing LoRA rank, learning rate, and two alternative architectures combined; capacity itself is a hard gate below which no data intervention helps. A single curation pass (shortening gold answers to canonical 1-6 word spans and dropping trivia) moved closed-book accuracy from 57.7% to 85.7% on a 15-document corpus, a larger jump than any architectural change. We confirm a capacity trend (rank must grow with corpus size) entangled with a coupling between rank and learning rate that we initially misdiagnosed. On a 15-document slice we add a real retrieval baseline: the internalized adapter (84.2% recall) beats a BM25-RAG pipeline with a base reader (58.9%) and even a realistic gold-chunk oracle (65.6%) at lower latency. We report the full arc, including three misdiagnoses, as a case study in debugging LLM training empirically.

</details>


### [62] [Leveraging External Knowledge for Historical Document Restoration via Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21936)
*Gabeen Kim,Kyeongpil Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ARI框架，利用大语言模型与检索增强生成技术来修复历史文献中受损的命名实体，在韩语历史文献上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于掩码语言建模的修复方法能利用局部上下文，但无法恢复需要外部历史知识的命名实体（如人名、地名等专有名词）。

Method: 将预训练LLM的隐式知识与检索增强生成（RAG）检索到的显式外部上下文相结合，构建ARI模型进行历史文档修复。

Result: 在韩语历史文献上大幅超越基线方法，在通用字符和命名实体修复上均有显著提升，并通过专家评估验证了实用性。

Conclusion: ARI可作为领域专家的实用工具，加速历史文献的分析工作。

Abstract: Historical documents act as invaluable knowledge archives but often suffer from illegibility due to physical deterioration and damage. While existing restoration methods based on masked language modeling effectively utilize local context, they struggle to restore named entities that require external historical knowledge. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel framework for historical document restoration that leverages large language models with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). By combining the implicit knowledge of pre-trained LLMs with explicitly retrieved external context, our model ARI effectively mitigates the challenge of inferring context-dependent proper nouns. Extensive experiments on Korean historical documents demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms baselines, achieving substantial gains in restoring both general characters and named entities. Furthermore, comprehensive evaluations including expert assessments confirm that ARI serves as a practical tool for domain experts, promising to accelerate the analysis of historical records.

</details>


### [63] [On Improving Faithfulness of Podcasts from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21961)
*Soumya Dutta,Tejas Indulal Dhamecha,Pannaga Shivaswamy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究文档导向播客生成中的忠实性问题，构建了跨5个领域、1500+文档的数据集，提出turn级LLM-as-a-judge评估框架，发现包括GPT-4o在内的先进模型频繁生成不忠实内容，并提出catch-n-repair框架检测并重写不忠实对话轮次，显著提升忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的播客等长对话内容虽流畅自然，但常引入无依据信息。现有研究缺乏对文档导向播客生成中忠实性的系统评估，因此需要构建评估方法和缓解策略。

Method: 1) 构建覆盖5个领域的1500+文档数据集；2) 提出turn级LLM-as-a-judge评估框架，通过人工研究验证可靠性；3) 提出catch-n-repair框架，检测并重写不忠实对话轮次，保持对话流畅性。

Result: GPT-4o等先进模型也频繁生成无依据内容；catch-n-repair在域内和域外场景均持续提升忠实性。

Conclusion: 文档导向播客生成的忠实性问题普遍存在，catch-n-repair框架能有效缓解该问题，且具有模型无关性和领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate long-form conversational content such as podcasts from textual sources. While these systems produce fluent and engaging narratives, they often introduce ungrounded information. In this work, we present the first systematic study of faithfulness in document-grounded podcast generation, where grounding must be maintained across conversational turns in long-form, multi-speaker transcripts. We construct a dataset of over 1500 documents spanning five domains and generate podcast transcripts using multiple LLMs. We introduce a turn-level LLM-as-a-judge framework for evaluating whether conversational turns are supported by the source document, and validate its reliability through human studies. Our analysis shows that even state-of-the-art models, including GPT-4o, frequently generate ungrounded content. To mitigate this issue, we propose catch-n-repair, a model-agnostic framework that detects and rewrites unfaithful conversational turns while preserving conversational flow. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in faithfulness across both in-domain and out-of-domain settings.

</details>


### [64] [Ground Truth First: A Longitudinal Evaluation Instrument for Agent Memory, and the Tenure Crossover in Memory-Architecture Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21962)
*Quentin Spencer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出逆向构建的LLM智能体记忆基准测试框架Veracium，包含~380个问题、15种类型，发现不同记忆架构在不同时间跨度下性能排名反转，并开源了相关工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆基准测试存在标签错误、数据污染问题，且主要评估短交互历史，缺乏对长时记忆和事实有效期的考量。

Method: 采用逆向流水线：先通过种子生命脚本采样器生成带有有效期、波动类别和来源通道的事实，再用LLM渲染器生成对话和邮件，用保真度验证器确认事实植入，最后机械实例化问题，确保黄金答案的脚本有效性。

Result: 五种记忆架构在两种时间跨度下排名反转：预算化策展映射记忆从三周96%降至九周72%，而来源类型化图谱记忆升至90%；完整渲染历史基线在短期表现最佳但九周无优势；写入质量与下游质量强相关；分层架构在两种场景均表现最佳（短期96.8%）。

Conclusion: 记忆架构的性能具有时间依赖性，长时记忆评估对智能体系统发展至关重要；Veracium作为开源工具可支持该方向研究。

Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-agent memory typically generate conversations first and extract answer keys afterwards -- with documented label-error and contamination problems -- and they overwhelmingly measure short interaction histories. We invert the pipeline: a seeded life-script sampler emits facts with validity intervals, volatility classes, and source channels before any text exists; an LLM renderer writes chat and email from per-event fact manifests; a fidelity verifier confirms every planted fact; and questions are instantiated mechanically from the script, so gold answers are script-valid by construction and separately validated for answerability. The synthetic, fictionalized corpus (~380 questions, 15 types) embeds features absent from the benchmarks we survey: per-fact validity intervals, sent/received trust distinctions, injection probes in a benign harness, and as-of-date question sets. Benchmarking five memory architectures against a no-memory control (fixed answerer, versioned LLM judge, three replicates, two horizons), we find backend rankings invert with history length: the budgeted curated-map memory that leads at three weeks loses recall of evicted content by nine weeks (96% to 72%) while a provenance-typed graph rises to 90%; the inversion is positive for all six users under complete cross-family re-judging (exact p=0.031). A full-rendered-history baseline ties or exceeds the best memory system at the short horizon but shows no judge-independent advantage at nine weeks, at about twice the read cost. Write-stage quality strongly correlates with downstream quality (weakly-written facts fail 24% vs 2%), and injection resistance tracked whether provenance boundaries survive representation. A layered architecture performs best among the memory systems in both regimes (96.8% short-horizon) and is released as Veracium, an open-source library, with the corpus generator and harness.

</details>


### [65] [MoE$^2$-LoRA: When MoE Models Meet MoE-style Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21978)
*Qingyu Yang,Haonan He,Minglei Li,Jingqi Ye,Tao Chen,Lei Bai,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MoE²-LoRA，一种用于MoE大模型的参数高效微调方法，通过双通道路由条件投影模块将预训练专家先验与任务自适应深度融合，并引入全局LoRA专家池实现跨层知识共享，在多个MoE骨干上取得SOTA下游性能并保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型的PEFT方法要么忽略路由器先验、使用统一适配器导致效率低下和遗忘风险，要么依赖静态专家选择、限制per-token容量和跨专家特征学习，因此需要更高效的自适应微调方案。

Method: 提出MoE²-LoRA方法，核心是双通道路由条件投影模块，重用基座路由器激活值来指导LoRA路由；同时引入跨层共享的全局LoRA专家池，实现全模型自适应和层次亲和力涌现。

Result: 在多个不同规模和专家粒度的MoE骨干模型上评估，MoE²-LoRA一致取得最先进的下游准确率，同时保持更强的通用能力。

Conclusion: 同时具备先验重用、动态适配器路由和模型范围知识共享三大优势，有效提升了MoE模型的参数高效微调效果。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have been widely adopted in large language models, yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for MoE models remains underexplored. Existing PEFT methods for MoE either ignore router priors with uniform adapters, reducing efficiency and risking forgetting, or rely on static expert selection, limiting per-token capacity and cross-expert feature learning. In this paper, we make the first attempt to fine-tune MoE models with MoE-style low-rank adaptation: our method, entitled MoE$^2$-LoRA, deeply couples the pretrained expert specialization with task-specific adaptivity via a dual-channel Routing-Conditioned Projection (RCP) module, which reuses base router activations to inform LoRA routing. We further introduce a single global LoRA expert pool shared across all layers, enabling model-wide adaptation with emergent layer-wise affinities and balanced expert utilization. MoE$^2$-LoRA simultaneously benefits from the advantages of prior reuse, dynamic adapter routing, and model-wide knowledge sharing. Evaluated on multiple MoE backbones with varying scales and expert granularities, MoE$^2$-LoRA consistently achieves state-of-the-art downstream accuracy while retaining stronger general capabilities.

</details>


### [66] [Analyzing Toxic Behavior and Its Impact on the Mastodon Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21980)
*Pasan Kamburugamuwa,Scrivner,Olga B*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究利用机器学习方法分析Mastodon去中心化社交网络中的有毒内容，揭示了毒性传播趋势及其对社区健康和去中心化治理的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mastodon作为去中心化联邦制平台，缺乏统一审核标准，生态多样且不均衡，这使得有毒内容的检测和缓解面临独特挑战。

Method: 采用机器学习方法对Mastodon用户的帖子进行分析，以检测和追踪有毒内容的传播。

Result: 阐明了Mastodon平台上的毒性趋势，揭示了毒性内容在去中心化环境中的传播模式。

Conclusion: Mastodon的去中心化特性给内容治理带来挑战，该项研究为理解毒性内容传播及其对社区健康的影响提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Mastodon as a decentralized federation of independently moderated social servers poses unique challenges for the detection and mitigation of toxic content. There are no unified moderation standards. The ecosystem is very diverse and uneven. This paper explores the development and spread of toxicity in Mastodon, utilizing machine learning methods to examine user posts. The results offer clarity on toxicity trends and its implications for community health and decentralized governance.

</details>


### [67] [J-CoT: Chain-of-Thought in J-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21981)
*Junde Wu,Jiayuan Zhu,Fengling Liu,Minhao Hu,Jiazhen Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出J-CoT，一种在词汇索引空间（J-space）上循环传递中间状态的新推理框架，无需完整的自然语言或稠密隐向量即可实现语言模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT方法受限于必须将中间状态完全用自然语言表达，而潜在推理方法虽免除此限制，但传递稠密隐向量时缺乏信息选择与组织机制。本文旨在设计一种介于二者之间的中间接口，既保持语言基础性又无需解码完整句子。

Method: J-CoT构建在J-space（词汇索引坐标系）上。每个循环中，模型在全隐空间计算；在循环边界，中间状态被表示为词汇索引系数（J-thought）进行传递，再映射回模型隐表示以进入下一循环。

Result: 在相同骨干网络和推理设置下，J-CoT-Zero在所有评估基准上达到或超过最强潜在推理基线；J-CoT-Train在数学、科学、编程及结构化路径推理任务中均取得最高分。

Conclusion: J-CoT在语言推理中有效平衡了表达灵活性与信息选择性，证明了无需完整自然语言解码即可实现高效循环推理的可行性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting improves language-model reasoning by carrying intermediate states across successive computation steps. However, relying on natural language as the only recurrent interface is overly restrictive, since many transient computations do not need to be fully verbalized. Existing latent-reasoning methods remove this constraint by recurrently propagating continuous hidden states. However, these methods pass a dense hidden vector as a whole, without an explicit mechanism for selecting and organizing the information needed by the next reasoning step. This motivates an intermediate interface that remains linguistically grounded without requiring a decoded sentence. We introduce \textbf{J-CoT}, a recurrent reasoning framework built on \emph{J-space}, a vocabulary-indexed coordinate system within the model's hidden representations. Within each cycle, the model computes in its full hidden space. At the cycle boundary, J-CoT expresses the intermediate state as vocabulary-indexed coefficients, carries these coefficients forward as a \emph{J-thought}, and maps them back into the model's hidden representation for the next cycle. J-CoT therefore requires neither a fluent intermediate rationale nor recurrence over the complete hidden state. Under matched backbone and inference settings, J-CoT-Zero matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated latent-reasoning baseline on every benchmark, while J-CoT-Train obtains the highest score across the evaluated mathematical, scientific, coding, and structured path-reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Analysing Self-Harm Representations in Language Models: a Cross-Architecture Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21988)
*Luis Espinosa-Anke,Carla Perez-Almendros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了LLM如何表示自伤内容，发现自伤信息在模型最后3-7%的层中结晶，且最准确的探针不一定具有最佳线性可分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自伤内容检测是NLP中的高难度、高风险任务，需要最高准确率以便及时干预或标记高危用户，因此理解LLM如何表示此类内容至关重要。

Method: 使用两个数据集（X-Sensitive和SH-Detection）和四个模型，进行两项实验：(1) 训练线性探针评估各层表示；(2) 提取对比自伤方向并进行归一化分析。

Result: 自伤信息在模型最后3-7%层（93-97%深度）结晶；最准确探针不一定最线性可分；Gemma-3-4B以更复杂的方式表示对比自伤方向。

Conclusion: 不同LLM对自伤内容的表示方式存在差异，这对自伤检测、LLM干预和监管具有下游应用意义。

Abstract: Self-harm content is particularly challenging to detect using NLP techniques, and is also a high-stakes task which requires the highest accuracy to enable timely intervention or flagging at-risk users. We therefore present an analysis of how LLMs represent such self-harm content, which has downstream applications in self-harm detection, LLM intervention and governance and policing. In this paper, we focus on two datasets and four models, and perform two main experiments: (1) We train and evaluate linear probes across all layers of each model on two self-harm datasets: X-Sensitive and SH-Detection. Across both corpora, self-harm information crystallizes in the final 3 - 7% of network layers (93 to 97% depth). (2) We extract contrastive self-harm directions and, after performing a normaliation step, we find that the most accurate probes are not necessarily the most linearly separable. In particular, we find Gemma-3-4B to represent this \textit{contrastive self-harm direction} in a slightly different, more intricate way than the other LLMs.

</details>


### [69] [DWT-Fusion: A Signal-Based Framework for Training-Free LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22026)
*Mehmet Batuhan Özdaş,Murat Osmanoğlu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DWT-Fusion框架，利用离散小波变换分析token级对数概率序列，通过多分辨率信号表示和校准加权投票融合，在零样本条件下有效检测LLM生成文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关检测方法依赖全局统计指标，忽略了token级可预测性中的局部和多尺度变化信息，这些信息对检测LLM生成文本可能有价值。

Method: 使用代理因果语言模型提取token级对数概率序列，通过离散小波变换进行多分辨率信号分析，提取局部概率动态特征作为检测信号，并设计四种训练无关投票融合策略（等权硬投票、等权软投票、校准加权硬投票、校准加权软投票）组合多个小波配置。

Result: 在HC3、M4、MAGE上，最佳单配置AUROC达0.9872/0.8185/0.7138，校准加权投票融合后提升至0.9919/0.8477/0.7471。

Conclusion: 基于DWT的多分辨率评分和校准引导投票融合为训练无关的LLM文本检测提供了有效且可解释的信号。

Abstract: Detecting LLM-generated text remains challenging under zero-shot and training-free conditions, especially when detectors must generalize across datasets, domains, and unseen generators. While existing training-free approaches exploit language-model statistics as detection signals, they typically characterize a text through global measures that summarize overall model behavior. Consequently, potentially informative local and multiscale variations in token-level predictability may remain underutilized. Motivated by this observation, we introduce DWT-Fusion, a training-free signal-based framework for detecting LLM-generated text using discrete wavelet analysis of token-level log-probability sequences produced by a proxy causal language model. The proposed framework analyzes these sequences through wavelet-based multiresolution signal representations and derives detection signals from localized probability dynamics. We further evaluate four training-free voting variants, including equal-weight hard voting, equal-weight soft voting, calibration-weighted hard voting, and calibration-weighted soft voting, to combine multiple wavelet configurations without training a supervised meta-classifier. We evaluate the framework on HC3, M4, and MAGE using GPT-Neo-2.7B, GPT-J-6B, Falcon-7B, and LLaMA-3-8B as proxy models. The best single wavelet configurations achieve AUROC values of 0.9872, 0.8185, and 0.7138 on HC3, M4, and MAGE, respectively. With calibration-weighted voting, the best ensemble variants further improve AUROC to 0.9919, 0.8477, and 0.7471. These findings show that DWT-based multiresolution scoring and calibration-guided voting fusion provide effective and interpretable signals for training-free LLM-generated text detection.

</details>


### [70] [Enough is as good as a feast: A Comprehensive Analysis of How Reinforcement Learning Mitigates Task Conflicts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22039)
*Zixuan Ren,Jinliang Lu,Junhong Wu,Yang Zhao,Dai Dai,Hua Wu,Haifeng Wang,Chengqing Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了强化学习（RL）和监督微调（SFT）训练范式对模型合并效果的影响，发现RL训练的模型在合并后性能下降更少、任务冲突更小，并从三个关键因素（on-policy数据、优化目标、正负例联合优化）解释了RL更适合模型合并的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并研究主要关注合并策略本身，但训练范式（SFT vs RL）对合并效果的影响尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过五个代表性任务的全面评估，对比RL训练和SFT训练的LLM在合并后的行为差异，并结合大量实证实验和理论分析探究原因。

Result: RL训练的模型在合并后任务冲突显著减少，性能下降更小，更适合模型合并过程。

Conclusion: 三种关键因素导致RL更适合模型合并：(1) on-policy训练数据控制梯度更新幅度更小；(2) RL优化目标（"够好即丰盛"）随着收敛逐步减少冲突参数更新；(3) 正负例联合优化引导模型进入无偏的任务特定参数子空间。

Abstract: Model merging plays a crucial role in consolidating multiple specialized models into a single, unified model, especially in the era of large language models (LLMs). Recent research has primarily focused on developing strategies to enhance merging performance with the trained models, while the impact of training paradigms, such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), on the effectiveness of model merging remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically explore the merging behavior of RL-trained LLMs compared to those trained with traditional SFT. Through comprehensive evaluations across five representative tasks, we find that RL significantly reduces task conflicts and results in less performance degradation after merging, making RL-trained models particularly well-suited for this process. To unearth the reasons behind the superior suitability of RL for model merging, we conduct extensive empirical experiments and theoretical analyses. Our findings highlight three key factors: (1) On-policy training data in RL control the gradient updates in a smaller magnitude, reducing the risk of overwriting existing knowledge for other tasks in the model. (2) The RL optimization objective, which favors ``\textit{enough is as good as a feast}", progressively reduces the magnitude and the number of conflict parameter updates as the model converges. (3) Joint optimization of positive and negative examples in RL steers the model towards an unbiased task-specific parameter subspace, ensuring robust performance while further preventing parameter conflicts.

</details>


### [71] [Developing and Validating the Spanish Version of the Large Language Models Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22041)
*Tran Gia Bao,Mo El-Haj,Sameha Al-Shakhsi,Antonio Garcia-Cabot,Raian Ali,Ala Yankouskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次验证了西班牙语版的大型语言模型依赖量表（LLM-D12-SP），该量表测量工具性依赖和关系依赖两个维度。通过对386名西班牙语参与者的分析，确认了量表的双因子结构、良好的内部一致性和外部效度，为跨语言研究LLM心理依赖提供了有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在组织和工作场景中的广泛应用，需要可靠且跨文化有效的工具来评估对其的心理依赖，特别是针对西班牙语人群（该群体LLM使用增长迅速但缺乏经过验证的心理测量工具）。

Method: 对386名西班牙语参与者进行LLM-D12-SP施测，采用验证性因子分析检验双因子结构，计算Cronbach's alpha评估内部一致性，并通过与网络成瘾、LLM可信度感知及认知需求的相关分析进行外部效度验证。

Result: 验证性因子分析支持了原始的双因子结构（工具性依赖与关系依赖）；量表整体内部一致性良好（α=0.89）；两个维度分别与网络成瘾和LLM可信度感知正相关，与认知需求弱相关或不相关，区分效度良好。

Conclusion: LLM-D12-SP是一个心理测量学上可靠的量表，结合已有的英语和阿拉伯语验证，为LLM依赖的跨语言研究提供了支持，有助于组织情境中LLM使用心理层面的研究。

Abstract: There is a growing need for reliable and culturally validated instruments to assess psychological dependency on large language models (LLMs), particularly as LLMs are increasingly used for task execution, decision-making, and communication in organizational and work-related settings. This need is especially relevant for Spanish-speaking populations, where LLM adoption is rapidly expanding, yet validated psychometric tools remain scarce. The present study reports the first validation of the Spanish version of the Large Language Model Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP), extending prior validations conducted in English- and Arabic-speaking samples. The LLM-D12 is a two-dimensional instrument assessing Instrumental Dependency (reliance on LLMs for performing tasks and supporting decisions) and Relationship Dependency (psychological reliance on LLMs for companionship and social interaction). A total of 386 Spanish-speaking participants (M = 28.0 years, SD = 6.1; 55% male) completed the LLM-D12-SP. Confirmatory factor analysis supported the original two-factor structure. The scale demonstrated good internal consistency (Cronbach's alpha = 0.89 total; 0.86 Instrumental; 0.85 Relationship). Discriminant validity analyses indicated that the two subscales represent related but distinct constructs. External validation showed that both dependency dimensions were positively associated with internet addiction and perceived trustworthiness of LLMs, while showing weak or no association with need for cognition. Together with prior English and Arabic validations, these findings establish cross-linguistic support for the scale's structure and provide a psychometrically sound tool for investigating psychological aspects of LLM use in organizational contexts.

</details>


### [72] [Scaling Native Multimodal Pre-Training From Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22043)
*Haoyuan Wu,Aoqi Wu,Hai Wang,Jiajia Wu,Jinxiang Ou,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了原生多模态预训练范式下的扩展规律，发现最小目标损失遵循可预测的计算定律，模型大小和token数量按幂律缩放，并且语言与多模态目标表现出不同的缩放行为。通过建模数据组合对计算定律的影响，推导出模型大小、token数量和数据混合的最优配置效率边界。下游评估表明原生多模态预训练能促进跨模态正向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在文本预训练中存在对多模态物理世界感知的限制，而原生多模态预训练虽有优势但缺乏对其扩展特性的系统刻画，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 在固定计算预算下研究transformer视觉-语言模型的最优模型大小和token数量，通过最小化目标损失拟合可预测的计算定律，分析语言与多模态目标的缩放差异，并建模数据组合对计算定律和分配指数的影响。

Result: 发现了最小目标损失的可预测计算定律和模型大小/token数量的幂律缩放；语言分配定律对数据组合不敏感，而多模态分配定律高度敏感；推导出了效率边界；跨模态正向迁移得到验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为可预测地扩展多模态基础模型奠定了实证基础，揭示了原生多模态预训练中不同目标的独特扩展行为以及数据组合的关键作用。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable reasoning capabilities, their reliance on text-only pre-training restricts the perception of the multimodal physical world. Native multimodal pre-training avoids this limitation by training models from scratch on multimodal inputs, thereby achieving deep cross-modal integration and mitigating optimization asymmetries inherent to traditional late-fusion architectures.
